# The RH Route–Literature Exchange Frontier: Literature through 9 August, Lean through 16 August 2026

## Abstract

This paper compares every proof, disproof, secondary, and retired route in the current
`HTGeometry`, `HilbertMetric`, and `RHTower` corpus with primary external literature available by
9 August 2026. It asks two questions route by route: what the formal corpus contributes beyond the
exact results located externally, and what external work contributes that is both absent from Lean
and capable of narrowing the route's first open source premise.

Local committed cutoff is
`cb9063536fbfc83e2554b18a4b4ea212bcaf5af9`, plus declaration-level review of current working tree
on 16 August. Historical `Active-tree Lean` wording records former provenance; current
scientific verdict follows declaration content and synopsis below rather than former cutoff labels.
Scientific
comparison follows declaration content rather than module placement.

Artin choice-free local factors, coefficientwise formal Euler source, virtual Frobenius reciprocity,

## 1. Standards and verdict language

Lean declaration types, not names or prose, determine local content. A result is credited as an
advance only when unconditional. An implication with an open premise, an RH equivalence, a theorem
assuming RH or simple zeros, and numerical evidence are classified separately.

“No collision located” means targeted comparison with primary sources found no theorem having the
same hypotheses and conclusion. It does not establish legal or historical priority. “Already in
Lean” requires a source-specific declaration or an exact logical/analytic connector; generic
functional analysis is not called a formalization of a paper's concrete operator.

Formalization is recommended only when an unconditional external theorem exists, is absent from
the Lean corpus, narrows a named open core, and is not merely another coordinate equivalent to RH.

“Every route” means every route instantiated or explicitly proposed in the audited corpus, not
every representation mathematics might later invent. Several corpus no-gos force changes of
primitive—pre-projection relation-valued currents, nonseparable prime/depth differentials, and
source-first chain descent—that are not exhausted by the classical route names used to organize
their parent literature. They are compared to their closest external languages without treating
those languages as definitions of what a proof route is.

## 2. Route-by-route exchange

### Route A — first clock [TERMINALIZED]

**Local contribution beyond located literature.** No external source was found proving that one
fixed published logarithmic clock is multiplicity-complete for every off-critical Xi divisor,
classifying its false-RH power/abscissa spectra, or reducing RH to its eventual lower floor and
Bohr-asymptotic source laws. This fixed-scalar tomography package is a plausible local contribution;
priority remains specialist-review dependent.
The concrete finite-aperture carrier is now exact in Lean: both the prime Dirichlet polynomial and
the cutoff Euler product have frequency module of rank `π(N)`, and the Euler-product Bohr hull
surjects onto the complete prime torus. Local guardrails also separate recurrence from weaker
properties: bounded holomorphy and differential algebraicity do not imply almost periodicity,
while one Euler factor is almost periodic with infinite support but rank-one frequency module.
None of this orients the published first-clock scalar. Fixed phase-natural source transport is
identity-rigid, and arbitrary aperture-dependent positive Markov feasibility is exactly the
original source sign; universal feasibility is exactly RH.

**External contribution.** CCM finite spectral triples and Connes's 2026 survey motivate the clock
and finite-to-infinite limit, but leave rigorous convergence open. In particular, Theorem 1.1 of
[Zeta Spectral Triples](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.22755) proves self-adjointness and the
regularized-determinant formula for the finite rank-one perturbation under its finite simple/even
ground-state hypothesis. The former conditional `CCMZetaSpectralTripleFinite.lean` packaging was
deleted after the preceding cutoff and receives no credit: placing the simple-even assumptions in
a Lean structure does not formalize their truth. Unconditional finite matrix, ground-shift,
determinant, resolvent, and real-rooted-completion infrastructure remains, but the literal
eta-channel assumptions and finite-to-infinite convergence are still absent.

**Lean action.** None as a Route-A advance. CCM Theorem 1.1 remains useful finite operator
infrastructure for Route O, but identifying another finite determinant coefficient or positive
transport cannot reopen this terminalized coordinate. A successor must orient the explicit signed
phase-connection defect by a genuinely new source theorem.

### CCM-FU — four-unit source-codiagonal incidence [OPEN / UNLETTERED]

**Local contribution beyond located literature.** This is not Route A's scalar first-clock
transport. Lean proves that the directed lower cutoff corner of the squarefree Fisher connection
has exactly half the two-sided commutator energy and hence four units in the critical integrated
limit. It also proves that the normalized critical channel converges weakly but not strongly and
that every fixed compact synthesis kills it. Independently, every internal Xi jet has a physical
`ell2(N;L2)` coface horizon before divisor evaluation. No external collision was located for this
joined one-corner/all-jet package or its exact Ward-incidence target.

The operator carrier foundations have advanced. Lean now constructs the full smooth
periodic star algebra, faithful multiplication representation, maximal `H1` Dirac domain,
commutator identity `[D0,M_a]=-i M_(a')`, domain invariance, bounded commutator, self-adjoint
compressed Dirac, and compact resolvent. Finite-stage field-one idele class space splits as
module-one compact part times `R_+^x`; Haar and `L2` split accordingly, and invariant `L2` is
exactly radial. These results do not canonically specify CCM's finite matrices `Pi(lambda,k)` or
orient their cofinal ground state. They are foundations, not the missing
four-unit transport.

The complete finite-stage field-one analytic carrier is now Lean-proved. Bruhat orbit counting,
rapid decay, periodization, angular/logarithmic `L2` density, and the literal Connes--Marcolli
Proposition 2.30 dense-range theorem are joined by Lemma 2.32's correlation/unfolding identity and
equation (2.252), which places theta differences in the source radical. Finite Fourier/Haar/
Plancherel, local and `p`-adic Bruhat products, self-dual Haar, and the full ramified Tate equation
(2.58) close the finite-stage Fourier/Tate carrier. The unit-invariant local Sonin space is
formalized exactly as the span of the spherical vector in Proposition 4.4, with radial Bruhat and
two-shell `L2` classification. Lean constructs the literal finite-stage physical aperture, the
semilocal common-kernel Sonin space of Definition 4.5, and the actual tensor Sonin vector `sigma_S`.
That vector is Fourier-fixed; its S-unit coefficients are explicit; `f tensor sigma_S` has exact
Fourier covariance, is angular-independent, and lands in the actual invariant subspace. For even
archimedean input, its full signed periodization collapses exactly to twice the positive Boolean
Euler cube; no infinite tail or hidden normalization remains. On a squarefree inverse-prime packet
`T`, its coefficient is exactly `mu(n_T)/n_T`, with every prime label retained, and finite Fourier
transport gives the same coefficient. The Boolean cube is canonically the powerset of selected
finite places, and its orbit sum is exactly finite Euler-product equation (4.23). This is a literal
finite-stage CCM--Route-E source seam.
`FieldOneFiniteStageThetaCoreEulerIdentification.lean` strengthens this from orbit algebra to the
physical finite-stage source: the literal theta-core logarithmic half-density equals exactly
`2 exp(t/2)` times the finite positive Euler value, with all prime labels and Boolean exponents
retained. `FieldOneFiniteStageThetaEulerSourceIdentification.lean` closes the operator-side
weld on the dense even-Schwartz carrier: every finite local factor is literally
`f(x)-p^-1 f(x/p)`, their product expands to the same Boolean Euler value, and the bounded Euler
operator sends the actual even-Schwartz source to that literal Schwartz product.

Common Haar normalization and the invertible finite Euler multiplier construct a candidate
whole-`L2` transport. `FieldOneFiniteStageThetaSourceNormalization.lean` exposes the forced
nonzero Haar/full-sign scalar, proves literal invariant theta core equals the corrected transport
on every even-Schwartz source, proves those sources dense, and proves literal cores dense in the
actual invariant class space. The corrected transport is therefore the unique whole-`L2`
extension of the literal construction, closing finite-stage source-faithful `isosonin`. It still

floor, or supply Route E's cofinal Hall/load/cost theorem.

The finite spectral implication has also advanced beyond the located even-ground literature.
`CCMParityFreeOddGroundFourier.lean` proves the odd simple-ground case from explicit finite block
data without assuming any spectral conclusion: the rank-two cross commutator, one-dimensional odd
radical, normalization, and nondegenerate frequency image force positive coupling; a
squared-frequency source completion is self-adjoint and nonnegative in the degenerate CCM metric;

`CCMCompactFourierMeanSquareZeroTransfer.lean` closes the generic analytic part of that last
passage. On a fixed interval, source mean-square error controls compact-Fourier error locally
uniformly on the complex plane. Hence transforms with only real zeros converge, whenever their
continuous sources converge in mean square, to a nonzero entire compact-Fourier limit with only
real zeros. This is stronger and more source-explicit than citing Hurwitz abstractly, but remains a
receiver: no theorem yet supplies the canonical arithmetic approximants, their required finite
block laws, their mean-square convergence, or their nonzero Xi identification.

`FieldOneSuzukiCriticalEulerHalfStepWeld.lean` supplies a second literal seam absent

### Route F — all-prime Sonin transfer [RETIRED]

frequency-side logarithmic shell channel is excluded for arbitrary tower heights under the exact
packet schedule hypotheses stated below.
Along the linear schedule, the corpus now excludes every typed scalar additive term `f(scale)` in the
typed `soninCompletedPacketValueVar` model with `f(r)/r → 0`, including unbounded logarithmic and
other sublinear contributions. It does not reduce arbitrary semilocal operators to that model.
The newer threshold theorem is stronger: it constructs `s>0` so every contribution eventually at
most `s r` fails on one admissible schedule, while a contribution restoring every admissible
schedule must exceed `s r` frequently. A uniformly bounded family of continuous linear operators
paired against a uniformly bounded vector sequence is reduced to a bounded scalar contribution and
therefore fails. The exclusion now covers unbounded channel families as well whenever their images

also has a uniform twelfth-order pointwise spectral tail and eleventh-order cutoff-energy tail.
Exact shell splitting proves height-independent frequency-channel bounds from
power tails. Choosing the least cut past twice the carrier then removes both height and cut index
from the rescaled-packet bound; its remaining data are base, ratio, carrier, half-width, packet norm,
and an outer inverse power of `δ(2w)`. These results do not reduce an arbitrary semilocal arithmetic
operator to the typed model. They do, however, close the actual Fourier logarithmic-shell family
for arbitrary tower heights under the exact packet conditions: carrier proportional to scale,
positive lower bound on half-width times twice carrier, bounded packet norms, and the stated
positivity/mean hypotheses. The full concrete Burnol conductor is now closed more directly: on
canonical packets its position-plus-reflected-frequency quadratic form is bounded by
`C₀ + C₁ log A`, uniformly over every finite tower height, and therefore fails to restore a lower
floor along the corpus's positive-linear source schedule for every height function.
Within Hilbert–Schmidt completions the local boundary is sharper: every sublinear norm envelope
fails, one explicit positive linear norm cap still fails, and any family restoring the scheduled
floor must exceed that cap infinitely often. Conjugating a fixed channel by arbitrary contractions,
including tower dilations and their inverses, leaves its contribution bounded and fails as well.
These are necessary size laws, not constructions.

**External contribution.** Burnol's
[complete/minimal systems paper](https://doi.org/10.5802/jtnb.434), Theorems 3.1–3.3, proves sharp qualitative
completeness/minimality laws for evaluator systems, Sonine evaluators including the critical

Connes–Consani's semilocal/scaling analyses provide a related finite-prime Sonin carrier. These
results can settle qualitative carrier closure but provide no quantitative convergence rate,

completion; those quantitative
layers are supplied locally, not by the cited papers.

**Lean action.** None for Route F. Burnol's qualitative completeness/minimality and co-Poisson

Schmidt property, and self-adjointness for `omega>1/2`. The Lean-proved CCM chain independently

literal bounded polar/prime remainder, canonical lower-bounded self-adjoint Friedrichs realization
with compact resolvent and true ground state, and aperture-antitone ground energy. These close
operator existence, not source selection of the complete ground space.

The 2012 differential chain is now complete in formal Lean. Appendix Ad2 supplies strong `L1`
aperture differentiation, regular-locus derivative continuity, and resolvent differentiation.
The coupled successor defines the two-sign boundary coupling, proves equations (410)--(413) in
strong `L1`, upgrades (413) to the literal pointwise equation on the joint arithmetic regular
locus, and derives pointwise (412). Together with (406)--(409), exact printed differential-system
parity is closed.

The continuation is now Lean-proved through Lemmas 4.7--4.11. It proves the continuation
formula, global physical kernel, original exterior-star integral, literal normalization and
completed parity, real-entire Schwarz symmetry, `mu` regularity, equations (4.14)--(4.19), the
determinant ratio and normalization derivative, and factorized/weak Schrödinger equations. These
remove the classical Suzuki-parity backlog but do not identify the source-selected restricted
resolvent or control its complete ground family.

New local theorems go beyond the located Suzuki statements without closing RH. The odd Donoghue
jet determines every inverse even moment and hence every even gapped finite measure, modulo the
formal dilation-invariant projective shape. A uniform Wiener--Neumann majorant produces an actual
two-sided convolution inverse and legal `ell1(L2)` carrier. An explicit translated-band example
falsifies unrestricted `L2(ell1)` order exchange, and a remote frequency band has divergent
physical `dr/r` energy. Conversely, the exact inverse-log grade hierarchy is nuclear in `L2(dL)`
for every finite oriented prime-power alphabet. The remaining source theorem is the uniform
word-coefficient majorant for the actual recursively forced solution plus its coupled boundary-
moment lock. An inhabited dyadic shift-cone obstruction is also unconditional on its defined edge
data, but identifying those data with Suzuki's literal restricted resolvent and selecting the
nonescaping full-jump Pick pair remain open. No exact external collision was located for this
combined determinacy/nuclearity/order-obstruction package.

The 2022 source parity audit is now exact and changes the backlog. Lean proves Theorem
1.1(i)--(iii), Titchmarsh shrinking zero gaps and Suzuki's uniqueness theorem, Theorems 1.2, 1.3,
1.5, 1.6, Proposition 3.1, `L1/L2` nonintegrability, and both the pointwise criterion and strict
positivity in Theorem 1.7. Theorem 1.5 is realized by the literal bounded-interval operator,
trace-class proof, and trace formula. Theorem 1.8 requires correction: Lean gives a sequence with
both determinant chains nonnegative but a negative shifted Hankel form. The valid generic
Stieltjes criterion is PSD of every ordinary and shifted Hankel matrix. RH implies the two Suzuki
determinant chains, but their special converse is not established. The weak first-gap theorem is
Lean-proved with the corrected missing `-(log pi)/2` term.
`SuzukiScrewFirstGapPositivity.lean` is also Lean-proved and proves the full printed existence of an interval strictly
beyond `log 2` by exact five-Lerch-block and telescoping-tail bounds, replacing the numerical root
search. The 2026 localized form/Friedrichs representation is Lean-proved.
Identification with the paper's named `A_a/B_a` objects,
simple/even ground selection, and deficiency/characteristic-function objects remain
literature-to-Lean backlog. Continuity of the actual CCM/Friedrichs lowest eigenvalue is now
Lean-proved and is not part of that backlog.

Published Theorems 4.2--4.3 have a separate form-domination issue. Lean formally refutes the
entrywise-kernel implication, constructs the oriented primitive, and proves both required
contour-shift and low-band inequalities for finite source packets. A Lean-proved module
constructs the primitive on the actual `L2(-a,a)` source class. Lean-proved
additions prove its finite energy, exact actual-source free-kernel Gram factorization, literal Eq.
(4.2) factorization through the angular Fourier transform, and both disputed form inequalities.
`SuzukiArchimedeanCoercivity.lean` also fixes the weight normalization and proves its global
minimum, high-frequency coercivity, and correct compact-band envelope. Lean-proved
`HTGeometry/FriedrichsFormOperator.lean` gives the generic positive self-adjoint form operator,
compact-resolvent implication, and core-extension theorem. Lean-proved
`SuzukiWeilRemainderRegularity.lean` proves that the literal 2026 smooth remainder has removable
value `-7/4`, installs its continuous even extension, and proves finite-aperture boundedness and
integrability. `SuzukiRestrictedWeilSourceForm.lean` then inserts Suzuki's
literal archimedean constant, exact visible prime-power aperture, and this kernel into the complete
four-block form. It proves the visible-prime block's exact jump-energy decomposition and lower
bound plus a uniform smooth-kernel difference bound. This removes every free source parameter and
localizes literal form semiboundedness to the finite-part block. Lean-proved
`SuzukiRestrictedWeilSmoothBound.lean` closes the functional-analytic estimate for the other
nonlocal source term: the exact smooth block is bounded below by an explicit finite multiple of
interval `L2` energy on every positive aperture. The subsequently committed
`SuzukiRestrictedWeilFinitePart`, `SuzukiRestrictedWeilSemibounded`, and
`SuzukiRestrictedWeilComplexCore` modules close the exact finite-part identity and full literal
real/complex smooth-core semibounds. `SuzukiLimitingLogFormCoercivity`,
`SuzukiPrimeFreeScaledCoercivity`, and `SuzukiScaledFormBridge` further prove explicit limiting-log
coercivity and strict small-aperture positivity of normalized source tests. Thus finite-part
semiboundedness and smooth-core positivity are no longer literature-to-Lean debts. The committed
CCM chain also closes exact-form completion and canonical Friedrichs representation. Remaining
2026 debt is comparison with the named `A_a/B_a` construction, simple/even ground selection, and
deficiency/characteristic objects; actual-ground continuity is closed.

Post-cutoff `SuzukiLimitingLogIrreducibilityCore.lean` proves the missing Markov calculation on the
literal real source core. Positive/negative decomposition has exactly one nonnegative cross-sign
term with kernel `1/|x-y|`; if both signs occur on nonempty open subsets, that term is strictly
positive. Therefore modulus contracts the finite-part energy and strictly contracts every
sign-changing core vector. This closes source-core irreducibility directly. Extending the ground
selection through the completed cofinal form and orienting its arithmetic source remain open.
Active-tree `SuzukiLimitingLogMixedReality.lean` independently closes the coefficient-level
complexification check: the limiting mixed closed form splits into restricted Weil form and
finite-prime, scalar, and origin-kernel corrections, and translated-prime plus raw/symmetrized

removes a phase ambiguity but supplies neither sign nor cofinal ground control.
Committed `SuzukiScrewNondegeneracy.lean` now identifies the literal interval form with its
operator pairing and proves nondegeneracy iff injectivity; `SimpleEigenvalueBranch.lean` supplies
the generic first-order simple-branch theorem. Lean-proved
`SuzukiBoundaryLayerHlogConvergence.lean` proves the analytic content of Suzuki's boundary
approximation (3.2) for the literal cutoff. `SuzukiBoundaryLayerFormCore.lean` then realizes
complex smooth tests, endpoint errors, and zero-extended aperture exponentials in the complete
`H^log` form domain and proves literal smooth-cutoff convergence in form norm.
`SuzukiCanonicalShiftedXi.lean` proves the exact 2012 equivalence between RH and real-rootedness of
every positive symmetric shifted-Xi function. `SuzukiCanonicalShiftRatio.lean` defines the literal
meromorphic `Theta_omega=E^omega sharp/E^omega`, proves regular-locus reciprocity and unit real
boundary norm, and identifies Suzuki's Hermite--Biehler condition exactly with regularity plus the

zero-free-half-plane/meromorphic-inner equivalence. `SuzukiCanonicalKernelMellin.lean` constructs
the literal compact incomplete-beta kernel and proves its Mellin transform is the required
archimedean-factor ratio. `SuzukiCanonicalArithmeticMellin.lean` now implements Suzuki's literal
inverse-Mellin dilation sources, their finite floor-truncated equations (204) and (a204), the
absolute-convergence and Tonelli/Fubini interface, and the exact Xi-ratio Mellin identities of
Proposition 1 and Proposition a1. Arithmetic Mellin source provenance is therefore closed; this
does not select the localized ground family. Lean-formal
`SuzukiCanonicalHankelOperator.lean` constructs the conditional spectral Hankel unitary and proves
equation (401) in `L2` and almost everywhere under the meromorphic-inner premise. Lean-formal
`SuzukiPositiveHalfLine.lean` transports this unitary to literal `L²((0,∞),dx)`.
`SuzukiCanonicalHankelCompressionBridge.lean` proves that the literal physical kernel operator on
`L²(0,a)` is exactly the finite compression of the canonical unitary and that compact-smooth log
sources are dense in the interval carrier. The same Lean dependency chain proves Suzuki's
Lemmas 4.3--4.6: compact output is zero; the finite section kills the reciprocal inner interval,
strictly contracts every nonzero vector, has norm below one, and both `1±H` are invertible; the
real finite and global Fredholm solutions exist and are unique. Appendix Lemma Ad1 is exact on the
regular positive locus, and the Ad2 chain proves strong `L1` and pointwise aperture derivatives.
Derivative-field continuity, equations (406)--(413), Lemmas 4.7--4.11, and equations
(4.14)--(4.19) are all formal; no 2012 parity debt remains.
The Lean-proved Abel stack culminates in
`SuzukiFinitePartLogFourier.lean`, which rigorously identifies the Abel-renormalized physical
difference energy with Suzuki's finite part and proves the literal finite-part Weil block equals
the logarithmic Fourier integral on the core, i.e. equation (2.6), without extra analytic premises.
These close literature interfaces, canonical-kernel provenance, transform regularization, and the
spectral/literal finite-compression identity. They do not prove the 2023 nondegeneracy converse or
Route G's complete-ground selection and lower control.

Committed `SuzukiOriginLogarithmicRemainder.lean` supplies the independent origin chart:
it proves the literal Eq. (2.2) decomposition of the source screw function, identifies the
renormalized remainder curvature, removes its singularity with value `-7/4`, and proves continuous
local boundedness. This closes source provenance for the small-aperture remainder; it does not
prove spectral perturbation, ground simplicity, or operator parity.
The literal external screw formula is now represented more concretely: Lean constructs `g`,
computes its regular kernel away from prime-power atoms, proves its exact plastic-number sign
threshold and atom-free distributional identity, rewrites the two-variable form as
cross-correlation, and constructs disjoint positive cross couplings. Hence no scalar `L²` shift
gives a Beurling--Deny positivity-preserving semigroup above the threshold aperture. Exact parity
decomposition gives `Q_even=T+H`, `Q_odd=T-H`, with `H` equal to a regular pairing minus a
finite explicit von Mangoldt prime-power sum. These close kernel and parity provenance, not the
cofinal operator limit.
Generic rectangular algebra now isolates the remaining Schur debt: a variable confined to one
matrix entry gives an exact degree-one determinant expansion, and coprime linear coefficients give
irreducibility. Multivariable reduction is also proved: an irreducible cofactor and a specialization
that kills it without killing the remainder imply irreducibility, including unit-signed corner
expansions. The reversed-variable rectangular Schur presentation now proves the height-one base,
corner-minor recursion, and unit-tail evaluations separating the current determinant from the
shifted smaller determinant. The induction is now assembled: over fields with decidable equality,
every universal rectangular Schur determinant with `0 < m ≤ n` is irreducible. Generic
irreducibility is closed; arithmetic specialization and source/Ward identification remain open.
At operator level, the corpus now proves strong convergence of the generic resolvent-coordinate
family for an injective self-adjoint bounded `B` with spectrum in `[0,M]`. It constructs neither the
unbounded source operator nor the identification `B=(A-ρ)⁻¹`, so it does not validate Suzuki's
conjectural finite-to-global spectral limit.

The safe-shift moment bridge has advanced materially. `SuzukiSafeBoundaryThetaCumulantWall.lean`
proves endpoint integrability, identifies the first three Xi boundary jets with mean `mu`, variance
`v`, and third cumulant `kappa_3` of the literal tilted theta law, and proves

```text
8(AC-B^2)=mu*kappa_3+(mu-v)(mu+2v).
```

It also proves `mu>v>0>kappa_3` and strict positive definiteness of every finite raw-moment Hankel
block. Thus boundary completion and cumulant calibration are closed. The sole remaining theorem at
this subgate is the theta-specific skewness budget
`-mu*kappa_3 <= (mu-v)(mu+2v)`. Generic moment positivity does not imply it, and even its proof
would not construct the cofinal two-angle producer.

`SuzukiMovableAnchorClarkCoherence.lean` gives the sharp positive survivor of real-axis
overcrowding. It derives the normalized Donoghue kernel, Nevanlinna atom weight, and Clark phase
speed, and identifies Clark probability with squared source coherence. Any finite orthonormal
family has total coherence at most one, so one of `N` extension modes has coherence at most
`1/N`; a fixed positive coherence floor bounds `N`. Cofinal orthonormal proliferation therefore
fragments a fixed source anchor. The remaining G mechanism must supply a source-derived
moving-anchor/two-angle coherence allocation, not more abstract self-adjoint extensions. No exact
external collision was located for this combined movable-anchor/source-budget statement.

The paper-level source-impedance audit removes coalescing as a second independent endpoint. The
theta source declares the impedance ratio `M_0(0)/M_0(1)`; solving the corresponding finite
Stieltjes equation selects the moving base, with canonical near-zero fallback. Once the original
fixed-anchor pointed probability converges, the selected base tends to zero and the hard-edge
Clark target follows automatically. Conversely, normalized finite defect vectors do not converge
strongly to a global source vector: their norms diverge and they weakly escape for every bounded
base. The fixed-anchor theorem must therefore be proved through bounded resolvent/Clark transforms,
not regular vector convergence.

`SuzukiTwoPortProjectiveWedge.lean` supplies a concrete projective mechanism. Common scalar
ground multipliers cancel in the exterior square. For antipodal ports
`beta=e^(iax)u+e^(-iax)v`, the aperture derivative of the two-port wedge has leading term
`-2ia (u wedge v)` plus four transport terms, with an exact lower bound when that term dominates.
This can retain transverse motion after scalar gauge cancellation. It does not prove that the
actual Suzuki Hardy ports are differentiable with the required remainder domination, so it is a
mechanism internal to Route G, not a new route or a closure of the fixed-anchor limit.

Dependency-closed paper audit moves the first gate ahead of occupancy. On the literal
half-interval forms `q_e=C+H_a`, `q_o=C-H_a`, put `K=C^(-1/2)H_aC^(-1/2)`. Midpoint reversal of
the exponential source gives the exact common-channel fidelity
`kappa_0=sech(a) chi_(a,lambda)`, `|chi_(a,lambda)|<=1`. Hyperbolic spectral calculus then forces
every cofinal Xi-admitted family to have negative normalized-Hankel edge
`liminf max(0,-inf spec K)>=kappa_Xi>77/85`. More sharply, if `r=-inf spec K`, `r_*` is the exact
edge required by the actual source fidelity, and `Delta_e,Delta_o` are the two exponential-source
bottom-edge deficits, then

```text
Delta_e + q Delta_o <= (1+q)(r-r_*)/(1-r^2).
```

Near-minimal admission therefore forces both literal source laws to concentrate on the bottom
edge and forces their prime-correlation surplus; exact threshold matching requires bottom-edge
multiplicity at least two. Three regimes survive: maximal edge, nonvanishing overshoot, or
near-minimal source-aligned edge. This is a source-admission classifier, not Suzuki closure or the
paired-occupancy theorem. These are dependency-closed paper results with no Lean proof.

The newest paper audit identifies both a positive global coherence reduction and its topology
wall. For physical prime reflections,

```text
D_a(f)=sum_n w_n ||f-R_(log n)f||^2
      =2 W_a ||f||^2-2 P_a(f,f).
```

An admitted negative edge bounds this Dirichlet energy only through its unused prime-mass reserve.
Since two reflections compose to translation by `log(n/m)`, the weighted mean square of all
log-ratio translation defects is at most `4W_aD_a`; small reserve would force delocalized prime
coherence. Edge strength alone does not make that reserve small. Moreover PNT cancellation is
strong but not uniform on each fixed endpoint window: the rescaled prime Hankel compression
converges strongly to the rank-one Laplace operator and the archimedean compression converges to
it in norm, yet their centered difference has positive cofinal operator-norm lower bound

```text
2(1-exp(-L/2)) sqrt((1-exp(-L))/L).
```

Finite atomic prime phases recur at aperture-dependent high frequencies. Unweighted operator norm
still does not construct the `77/85` edge because the common finite-part Cauchy form charges those
frequencies. Route G's first gate is therefore a relative bound for the centered critical Mellin
discrepancy in that logarithmic form topology, or a source-aligned cofinal edge construction—not
another leading PNT asymptotic. These theorems are paper-level and have no Lean proof.

Two further paper theorems close negative-shift recession as a complete-family escape. At the
macroscopic scale `-lambda_j/e^(a_j)->c>0`, any fixed `e^a` coercivity margin below both parity
floors makes the two normalized literal source resolvents converge to the same value `1/(8c)`, so
`kappa->0`; Xi admission then requires macroscopic floor hugging. A complete Xi-identifying family
cannot use that singular alternative, because its own target identification implies RH and hence
nonnegative parity floors. More strongly, after complete identification the exact exponential
sources have bounded parity Rayleigh means, and Jensen's resolvent sandwich yields

```text
limsup max(0,-lambda_j) <= 4M_0(1)/(R_Xi-1) < 16M_0(1)/77.
```

The last argument deliberately uses the RH conclusion of complete identification; it is a
necessary schedule classification, not an unconditional admission producer. The former claimed
complete-family rate `r=O(log(a)^4/a)` is retracted: its low-block Gram constant grows with the
cutoff, so the stated bound did not follow. What remains valid is `r->0`, together with the
low-only ceiling `r=O(a^(-1/8)log(a+2)^(1/4))`; no comparable schedule rate is proved for the
high-assisted branch.

`HTGeometry/RidgeMatchedCoherenceBarrier.lean` now replaces that invalid rate
by an exact source-specific classification. Any high-assisted survivor has both unregularized
low/high energies above `r/14`, must place a definite coefficient fraction in a polynomial
ordinate collar, and splits after Schur whitening into direct-tail amplification or spectral mass
of `TT*` generated by the literal low source. Cross-capacity and tail scales formally contradict
the direct-tail alternative. Hence the sole high-assisted branch is source-cyclic, with

```text
mu_w((1/113,1]) > r/(128 C_T) >> r/log(1/r)^2.
```

This lower mass is formal. The open theorem is the opposite source-specific upper bound, or a
construction that genuinely realizes mass at that scale; generic small singular values and
high-block coercivity are both closed as substitutes. Exact first gate is therefore the singular
zero-shift source-cyclic collar, not a generic bounded finite-shift chart.

Current working-paper Theorems AG--AJ classify that boundary layer further. Any successful
negative complete family forces both literal parity spectral laws weakly to `delta_0`, more than
`73/81` of odd probability into `(r,a^(-1/2))`, and the full transport cost
`log R_Xi>log(81/4)` into every free probe interval `(r,epsilon)`, with an inverse-width hazard
peak. Yet exact paired Rayleigh energy stays nonzero and escapes to spectral infinity through
vanishing probability; the combined second moment diverges. Thus weak hard-edge collapse,
one-channel Stieltjes bounds, first-moment coupling, uniform integrability, and every finite-moment
closure are nonterminal or false in the needed direction. Remaining source theorem must couple
infrared parity transport to ultraviolet escape through relative prime--Gamma Hankel geometry.
The size-biased theorem locates that ultraviolet layer: after scaling energy by `2a`, it
converges to the inverse-square Xi zero probability pushed through the zero-multiplicity map, so
order-one energy sits at `2a*m_gamma+o(a)` with probability `Theta(1/a)`.
These additions are paper-proved and have no Lean proof.

The paper audit linearizes the remaining singular law using both canonical extension
spectra. Compactify each positive interlacing even/odd pole--zero gap by
`x=(1+t^2)^(-1)` and put Lebesgue measure on their disjoint union. The exact exponential-product
identity expresses the fixed-anchor Herglotz transform as the Stieltjes transform of this
`[0,1]` occupancy measure. Consequently the fixed-anchor pointed law is equivalent to weak
convergence of compactified even/odd spectral-shift occupancy, or equivalently to convergence of
every paired smoothing-trace difference. Nearly cancelling polluted pole--zero pairs contribute
only their compactified gap length; regular convergence of individual defect vectors is not
needed.

The zeroth occupancy moment is the source defect `(1-kappa_a)/2`. On every sufficiently large
aperture with a source-visible simple even ground state, continuity between the free value
`2a/sinh(2a)` and the ground value `1` selects a shift matching the unconditional theta/Xi
constant `kappa_Xi`, without divisor fitting. This does not select source-visible odd ground and
does not control higher moments. For the compactified occupancy lane, exact frontier remains full
paired occupancy/all-smoothing-trace convergence to the theta-declared measure. For the forced
negative zero-shift lane, frontier is instead the infrared/ultraviolet prime--Gamma coupling
above. One extension spectrum, matched zeroth moment, or finite moment budget is insufficient.

The literal operator and exterior carriers have also advanced beyond generic operator existence.
`SuzukiWeilFriedrichsOperator.lean` is now Lean-proved through the `H_0^1` form-domain chain.
It constructs the actual interval restricted-Weil operator from the complete source form and proves
self-adjointness, compact resolvent, and a ground eigenstate. The form core, operator-domain
inclusion, Dirichlet screw factorization, equation (2.5), exact CCM source identification, and
maximal adjoint-domain classification are formal. `SuzukiApertureL2Equivalence.lean` supplies the
literal interval/zero-extension unitary. Operator formation and literal restricted-resolvent
provenance are therefore closed.

The natural product is now identified at theorem level. The complex screw equals the
complexification of the real source screw; a literal `L2` adjoint witness has reverse primitive
equal to the zero-mean screw compression; every compressed screw output lies in the maximal
Dirichlet-adjoint domain; and

~~~
Dom(D†G_aD)=H_0^1(-a,a).
~~~

Lean also computes `D†G_aD` on that domain as the explicit adjoint witness. `SuzukiScrewOperatorSymmetry.lean` proves the same natural product is formally self-adjoint there.
These results close Suzuki's hard domain inclusion, natural-domain equality, action, and symmetry
interfaces. They do
not orient the ground floor.

On the exterior side, every `C^1` positive-half-line homogeneous Gamma-channel solution is uniquely
`eta^(-1/2)Q(eta)c`. Variation of constants proves bounded forcing contributes `O(eta)` and no
half-log coefficient. The full two-ray inverse Fourier integral now has exact asymptotic
`2*pi*L^(-1/2)*beta+O(L^-1)` uniformly in truncation; `beta=0` is the terminal-`L2` branch, the
scattering map is onto, and the corona quotient is exactly one trace-space copy. Whole-frequency
assembly is closed. The point-support programme now proves the complete finite-dimensional
structure theorem: an origin-supported tempered distribution is a finite delta-derivative
combination

~~~
u(phi)=sum_(j<=N) phi^(j)(0)c_j.
~~~

Lean computes the principal Gamma action and proves each coefficient is multiplied by
`2j+1`; the principal operator has no nonzero point-supported kernel. It also proves abstract
scalar and operator-valued lower-triangular jet systems vanish whenever all diagonal maps have
trivial kernel. `PointSupportGammaForm.lean` closes the source-specific atom calculation:
an origin-supported tempered distribution satisfying

~~~
u(phi)+2u(eta phi')+i u(g phi)-i T(u(eta phi))=0,
~~~

with `g(0)=0` and bounded `T`, is zero. Remaining step is not generic distribution classification,
triangular algebra, or full-equation atom reduction. `CompactPrimitive.lean` also closes the
generic interval lemma: derivative-zero distributions are locally constant multiples of Lebesgue
measure. `SuzukiGammaDistributionalHalfLine.lean` now completes the conjugation and patches local
vectors: every weak solution is one `|eta|^(-1/2)Q(eta)c_+` channel on the whole positive ray and
one such `c_-` channel on the whole negative ray. It also contains the no-origin-supported-solution
capstone. It remains to realize both ray profiles globally as tempered distributions, subtract
them from the physical source, and prove the residual is supported at zero and retains the exact
weak equation. `SuzukiFlatFluxChargeAutomatic.lean` separately makes flat terminal charge zero and
rules out a bounded nonzero `1/log` endpoint dipole; these are no longer matching premises.
Active-tree `SuzukiLiteralPairedSourceAssembly.lean` proves the exact left/right rooted valuation
signs, centered prime-descendant decomposition, scale-buffered cutoff support, and paired primitive
identity with no derivative on event sources. This closes literal source algebra and support
provenance, not identification of the complete source with a physical weak-derivative pair,
completed forcing, global tempered subtraction, or trace orientation.
The now-formalized paired-source package adds both event windows of exact width `log 2`, forced
root behavior, uniform cutoff derivative bound on its support, uniform `ell2` source control,
summability of every finite-recurrence polynomial clock under the strict slope gap, and extension
of coordinate identities to whole finite-`p` carrier. Thus exact two-coset source equation and
finite-recurrence admissible class are closed. Remaining exterior science is arbitrary finite-order
expansion, isolation of all-orders-flat residual, finite-type trace-zero `H1_0`, recurrence-infinite
apertures, global tempered subtraction, raw Evans positivity, and oriented return.
`SuzukiRootRetainedProjectedSource.lean` closes abstract projection bookkeeping: from a full
root-retained equation, projection commuting with jump and differentiation produces interior
equation forced exactly by exterior derivative, and projected source is `L2` whenever that
derivative is `L2`; Schur complement is recovered only afterward. No theorem yet instantiates this
carrier with completed all-prime Suzuki source, so it does not close source realization,
all-orders-flat classification, or oriented return.
`SuzukiRootRetainedEnergyEstimate.lean` adds exact forced-difference algebra and unweighted
coercive estimate `(mu-tau)||E'||<=||forcing||`, with explicit inverse-gap form above
`2L_0>tau`. It remains generic: source-specific retained carrier, jump forcing bound, and cofinal
return are not instantiated.
The formal retained-carrier package now additionally proves grade-one `L^-3/2` projected decay,
first/second derivative power bounds, `H1(dL)`/`L2(dL)` admission, affine-Gamma-jump mapping from
one Fourier derivative to `L2`, exact projected exterior forcing, dyadic terminal smoothness, and
weighted subcriticality of low germs. Active-tree scattering-charge necessity proves a finite
`L2` remainder cannot cancel a non-`L2` leading head. These close terminal analytic admission.
They do not identify actual completed zero modes with the retained family, establish exact global
cutoff intertwining, give trace-zero weighted exponent above `1/2`, or prove two-port return
injectivity/orientation.
`RealL2LipschitzWeakDerivative.lean` removes the generic differentiability gap: a globally
Lipschitz real `L2` profile with `L2` classical derivative and vanishing endpoints has that literal
derivative as its complex tempered-distribution derivative. Active-tree
`RealL2SmoothOnsetTail.lean` proves the canonical smooth activation turns every bounded
exponentially decaying Lipschitz tail with matching derivative decay into such a whole-line `H1`
pair. `SuzukiOnePrimePolarSource.lean` now instantiates that theorem for the literal
endpoint-centered polar trace: explicit exponential value and derivative envelopes, global
Lipschitz control, `L2` onset primitive and derivative, and exact tempered weak differentiation.
The remaining premise is no longer one-prime polar membership; it is completed all-prime/all-
aperture assembly with the physical cutoff/jump source and singular Gamma contribution.
Active-tree `SuzukiGammaStripBranch.lean` proves the scalar ratio
`Gamma(1+iz)/Gamma(1-iz)` has a nonvanishing holomorphic square root on `|Im z|<1`, normalized at
zero. `SuzukiGammaStripGluing.lean` constructs the homogeneous vector-valued strip solution and
proves its positive/negative channels glue iff `c_-=-i*c_+`, equivalently iff the half-log endpoint
trace vanishes. `HTGeometry/ExpSelfAdjointNormBound.lean` proves only the real part of a complex
self-adjoint exponent contributes to norm growth, and `SuzukiGammaStripSquareIntegrable.lean`
uses the exact integrable Gamma carrier to prove the glued solution is `L2` on every interior line.
This closes homogeneous gluing and interior-line integrability, not the source theorem forcing that
trace condition after completed two-ray assembly. `SuzukiGammaStripCarrierTwoSided.lean`,
`SuzukiGammaStripContourPairing.lean`, and `SuzukiGammaStripFourierWeight.lean` strengthen this:
the carrier has matching power upper/lower bounds, line pairings are height-independent, and one
measurable Fourier profile represents all interior heights with the exact exponential `L2`
weights. `SuzukiGammaWallCutoffCommutator.lean`, `GammaJumpCommutatorKernel.lean`, and
`GammaJumpNearDiagonalLipschitz.lean` prove wall/jump commutator bounds with integrable kernel
`C |chiHat(t)|(|t|+t^2)` and Young `L2` control on the `H1` side.

diagonal radial spectrum. Generic off-diagonal/BV/high-row summation and abstract assembly are now
formal; literal normalized constrained-prolate dictionary remains. These results control selected
seed, not true restricted-Weil ground space.

`CCMPublishedRestrictedWeilParityBlocks.lean` reconstructs the literal centered packet of the
published finite matrix, proves exact PSD even/odd blocks and rank-one cross commutator, and shows
the shifted floor is PSD and singular at every aperture. A simple full odd radical automatically
supplies the visibility and normalization needed by the parity-free real-zero theorem. The
`CCMRestrictedWeilMixedSourceForm`/`SuzukiCCMMixed*` chain also proves the exact complex
sesquilinear source form and its two-sided polar--prime minus archimedean Weil identity. Active-tree
`SuzukiCCMMixedScrewWeilBridge.lean` identifies that form, for every strict complex source, exactly
with the compressed Suzuki screw pairing and Suzuki's actual Xi-zero Weil distribution. No located
external theorem supplies simple-ground selection, finite-to-Xi convergence, or whole-ground lower
control; these formal results close packet, mixed-form provenance, and the CCM--Suzuki operator
bridge only.

The finite spectral algebra and its analytic operator limit are sharper still.
`ConstrainedProlateTwoModeRayleigh.lean` and `ConstrainedProlateLadderRayleigh.lean` compute exact
Rayleigh quotients on orthonormal diagonal mode ladders and prove their normalized leading-scale
limit when lower leakage ratios vanish. Besides `8/11` and `8/13`, Lean obtains normalized gaps
`3/11` and `5/13` and first adjacent Hermite determinant ratio `242/211`. `ArithmeticDilationWeightedProjectiveDischarge.lean` proves that an inverse-bandwidth off-diagonal
envelope against a summable row majorant plus a dominated pointwise-vanishing diagonal defect
forces weighted projective convergence; the existing trace-class realization upgrades this to
Schatten trace-norm convergence toward the positive diagonal arithmetic operator. No external
theorem is needed after those concrete estimates are available. `ConstrainedProlateNormalizationAudit.lean` cross-verifies the normalization chain: total limiting
leakage mass is `4/11`, arithmetic eigenvalue is `2 log(k)w_k`, operator trace is the negative prime
constant, and Dirichlet divisor response is exactly one half of that trace.
`ProlateTwoModeRadialLeakage.lean` now verifies physical diagonal and dominated weighted sum
directly, without a false a.e.-density premise. Corpus still lacks the literal source dictionary
closing concrete seed certification and, independently, whole-ground comparison needed for RH.
`HTGeometry/NonstationaryPhaseLowerBound.lean` supplies generic first-derivative test giving
`O(1/(c*gap))` once phase-speed gap and amplitude variation are verified.
`RadialDilatedPhaseSeparation.lean` now instantiates phase half: actual dilated prolate speeds differ
by at least `(2/3)(j-k)` for `j>=k>=2`, while sum speed is at least `j+k`, uniformly in radius and
turning parameter. `RadialPSWFAmplitudeVariation.lean` closes endpoint, Gronwall, and total-variation
control in interaction-picture variables. `RadialTwoIndexOscillatoryBound.lean`,
`RadialSymbolTwoBranchBound.lean`, and `RadialNormalFormPotentialDecay.lean` then give explicit
two-branch off-diagonal bounds with both inverse first-gap and inverse-square second-gap decay.
Remaining certification is concrete instantiation by the normalized radial PSWF symbol with
uniform index constants. `RadialSymbolTwoBranchInstance.lean` closes the exact exterior
normal-form instance under named interaction-total and potential-decay bounds;
`CCMHighRadialFirstRowBV.lean` closes high-row BV, weighted summability, and vanishing shifted
logarithmic tail; `RadialPrimeChargeAssembly.lean` closes abstract head-plus-three-errors assembly.
Literal normalized-source dictionary remains, but row/projective analytic mechanism is formal.
This is distinct from RH-bearing whole-ground step.

Latest modules expose why whole-ground conjecture must be two-scale.
`CCMRadialGlobalDefectMass.lean` proves exact defect-mass transport and shows old outer coordinate
`lambda^2(x-1)` carries no limiting mass. `CCMRadialTwoScaleCarrier.lean` proves endpoint slope has
`lambda^3` scale while total mass has `lambda` scale, with exact `lambda^2` ratio. A one-scale
relative normal form is therefore incomplete. `ProlateProfileDecay.lean`,
`ProlateOscillatorLadderPin.lean`, `ProlateDefectTransfer.lean`, and
`ProlateCentreValuePairing.lean` derive global decay, exact Hermite oscillator ladder pin, derivative
transfer, and centre-pairing pinning. They open a concrete path to profile exhaustion but do not
prove that every restricted-Weil near-ground sequence decomposes into oscillator-pinned ladder,
two boundary scales, prime-phase escape, and coercive bulk.
Active `HTGeometry/OscillatorMassNoEscape.lean` closes generic tightness step: unit nonnegative
Hermite mass under one uniform oscillator first-moment bound cannot escape to high indices, and
coordinatewise vanishing off one rung forces mass one on that rung. The missing source theorem is
sharpened by active `HermiteCoefficientPin.lean`: bounded off-rung defect pairings plus ladder pin,
escaping bandwidth, unit mass, and uniform oscillator energy force every off-rung coefficient to
zero and selected coefficient square to one. Missing source theorem is derive these bounds/pins
uniformly from actual restricted-Weil near-ground equation; generic saturation does not supply it.
Active `SecondMomentIntegralTail.lean` gives exact companion tail control: uniform second moment
forces far integrals to decay as `K^-1/2`, so local/norm convergence can pass through total pairing
once actual family proves uniform moment bound.

`RadialPSWFArithmeticDilationDischarge.lean` closes the diagonal target for the explicit
Dunster leading arclength density. For each fixed `k>=1`, its exterior tail tends to
`8/(11*pi*k) * arcsin(1/k)` as phase frequency diverges and turning parameter vanishes; multiplying
by `2 log(k)` gives the exact constrained-prolate arithmetic eigenvalue. This proves the target is
source-derived at leading-density level. `ProlateTwoModeRadialLeakage.lean` upgrades diagonal to
actual physical PSWF and supplies uniform inverse-square domination; remaining seed certification
is literal source instantiation, not another diagonal or generic summed-projective estimate.

`ProlateRadialDecay.lean` derives the exact `1/y` radial tail directly from the ODE, transports it
to the logarithmic half-density channel, proves `k^-2` channel mass bounds summable, and bounds each
polar charge. Active-tree `RadialLatticeSourceEnvelope.lean` applies the same half-density law to
the literal compact-BV lattice source, with squared mass `O(1/lambda)` and one remaining primitive
bound. `SuzukiRayleighInfimum.lean` proves the exact restricted-Weil floor is attained and equals
the infimum over strict-interior smooth sources. These are local corpus advances beyond the
located prolate/CCM literature. They remove radial decay and ground existence/smooth visibility,
and later modules remove physical diagonal comparison and uniform summed-channel primitive control.
They do not select attained cofinal ground.

The operator/seed frontier has moved further. Lean identifies the interval floor with the actual
CCM/Friedrichs ground, proves continuity for every positive aperture, eventual small-aperture
positivity and an explicit two-sided `O(a)` asymptotic, and realizes the limiting logarithmic form
as a closed self-adjoint compact-resolvent operator with attained ground. On its literal real core,
the limiting form has an exact Beurling--Deny modulus law and strict energy loss for sign-changing
vectors. Global band extension, exact Slepian self-reciprocity, finite even joint-prolate
decomposition, and a live two-mode zero-mean source require no simplicity premise. The radial Abel
boundary equals the full source, with uniform Abel bounds, limit/interchange, and exact channel
split. Post-cutoff the Gaussian trial satisfies the strict half-separation inequality for all
`c>=400`; `ProlateEvenEigenspaceQuadratic.lean` proves that any such witness already in a live even
band eigenspace selects a genuine smooth joint prolate mode. These are corpus-specific formal
connections beyond the located numerical CCM literature. `ProlateBandHilbertSum.lean` constructs
every band component and their unconditional sum, while `ProlateReflection.lean` and
`ProlateBandComponentEven.lean` prove reflection compatibility and that every nonzero-eigenvalue
component of a continuous even profile is itself even. Projection and parity are therefore not
bridges. `CompactFourierIntegrableEntire.lean` also proves the generic injectivity tool: an
integrable compactly supported amplitude whose compact Fourier transform vanishes on a
nondegenerate real window is zero almost everywhere. Newer route-specific modules close former
band-component and seed bridges: `ProlateBandOperatorInjective.lean` removes zero component;
`ProlateLowSeparationMode.lean` selects a nonzero even joint mode with separation ratio below one
half for every `c>=400`; `ProlateUnconditionalChannelBound.lean` sums all radial channels.
`ProlateMeanZeroSource.lean`, `ProlateChannelPrimitiveBound.lean`,
`ProlateSourceVariation.lean`, and `ProlateSourcePolarCharge.lean` construct explicit compact
mean-zero source and prove full primitive and polar-charge realization unconditionally. Actual-
PSWF comparison and channel transport are no longer open. Sole RH-bearing bridge is control of all
cofinal restricted-Weil ground directions rather than one selected prolate sector.

The completed-Evans/log-seam producer is a second internal Route-O branch. Newly formal
`SuzukiGammaWeakDomainUpgrade.lean` proves a supported weak tested Gamma equation yields the
required `L2` derivative and `H1` profile under the explicit numerical-range budget, so the two
derivative-scattering charges are no longer separate conjectures. `SuzukiRootPortObservationGram`
proves an exact scaled-isometry observation law and excludes every nonzero dark endpoint trace once
`a>(log 2)/2`. Its first open theorem is now source realization: map the literal completed zero
mode into the support-pruned normal carrier with square summability and exact cutoff/compressed-
adjacency intertwining. Then remain trace-zero forcing and raw Evans injectivity. No located
external theorem constructs that source-specific intertwiner. These results do not control the
prolate whole-ground floor and do not create a new route.

The latest oscillator chain corrects scope of preceding profile claims. Limiting ODE rigidity is
unconditional:
`HTGeometry/OscillatorSpectralRigidity.lean` proves every nontrivial real solution of
`v''=(x^2-mu)v` with integrable square has `mu=2n+1`, using formal energy/product monotonicity,
recessive limits, boundary vanishing, and lowering identity. `ProlateRescaledCoefficients.lean` and
`ProlateProfileWindowLimit.lean` give exact normal form, fixed-window coefficient errors, and
conditional Grönwall convergence; `ProlateSecondMoment.lean` gives global moment control;
`ProlateGaussianSharpRayleigh.lean` closes sharp `n=0` trial primitive. The formerly missing step is
now closed for ground mode: `ProlateSharpSeparationMode.lean`,
`ProlateApertureVirial.lean`, and `ProlateGroundGaussianLimit.lean` prove an actual even joint
prolate family has `mu_c<1`, sharp lower bound `c<=mu_c(c+3)`, `mu_c->1`, and normalized Gaussian
defect tending to zero. `ProlateNormalizedProfile.lean` now discharges centre normalization and
the rescaled ODE for a genuine even joint mode; `ProlateGaussianWindowLimit.lean` and
`ProlateWindowMass.lean` give Gaussian profile/window-mass convergence. `ProlateGroundTotalMass.lean`
derives the uniform phase and second-moment bounds, proves normalized total mass tends to
`sqrt(pi)`, and closes the centre-mass asymptotic unconditionally for the selected band family.
`ProlateGeneralTrialMode.lean` extends variational mode extraction to every smooth even trial, and
`ProlateHermiteRungWindowLimit.lean` gives each fixed Hermite-window limit once its separation
constant is pinned to `2n+1`. `ProlateEvenEigenspaceRelativeBound.lean` and
`ProlateRelativeTrialBound.lean` prove complement Rayleigh inequality needed for min--max counts.
`HermiteFunctionOrthogonality.lean` adds exact finite-window Wronskian identity and vanishing
cross-rung inner products. Missing pieces are rung-wise prolate Rayleigh bounds, band-class
independence/multiplicity, cross-spectral ordering, and rung index.
Immediate certification residue is cross-spectral ordering identifying that family with signed
Fourier mode zero, not analytic profile or total-mass control. `CCMProlateEndpointJet.lean` also
proves fixed endpoint-jet truncations are noncofinal.
Remaining scientific theorem then proceeds to higher fixed-depth min--max ladder/rung-index
identification and whole-ground exhaustion. No located PSWF literature provides this exact
constrained source-indexed theorem ready for import.

`CCMLimitingRadialWeber.lean` fixes bulk radial critical point/curvature, proves endpoint Weber
phase nonstationary, and sums logarithmically weighted first row as `O(c^-1/2)` under supplied
`k^-2` row bound. Actual Dunster/Bessel realization, stationary phase, and Weber transform remain
outside theorem, so this advances source dictionary but not whole-ground control.
`CCMRadialPrimeFirstRow.lean` now makes first-row reduction exact: finite prime charge splits into
diagonal, first row, and high sector; truncated von Mangoldt charge is nonnegative and dominated by
`log k`; coalescing stationary point is unique with exact curvature and `1/(2k^2)` endpoint scale.
Literal Dunster expansion, phase-preserving Gram estimate, stationary phase, and Weber--Abel
transform remain unformalized.

**External contribution.** Connes–Consani–Moscovici's
[Zeta Spectral Triples](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.22755) gives concrete finite-prime self-adjoint
rank-one perturbations and numerical spectral/determinant convergence; Connes–van Suijlekom prove
real zeros for the Fourier transform of an isolated simple even ground state. Rigorous convergence
of the concrete family would prove RH and remains open.

**Lean action and frontier.** Finite matrix/reflection/real-root completion, low-separation prolate
selector, compact/exterior radial primitive, and polar-charge seed law are formalized. Scientific
frontier is not another seed asymptotic, prolate leakage ground calculation, radial prime tail,
channel estimate, or numerical finite-to-Xi convergence: it is a
source-derived classification of every near-ground sequence into prolate, translated two-edge,
prime-phase, and coercive-bulk channels, with intact polar-prime cross terms and relative bulk
coercivity. Only after this whole-ground exhaustion can one orient the surviving boundary channel
or prove the true restricted-Weil floor nonnegative.

### Route P — completed-seam Thorin/Markov carrier [OPEN]

**Local contribution beyond located literature.** The corpus isolates a source-pure compact
Thorin/Markov carrier and an infinite-support moment consumer, while proving finite-moment and
unlocked-carrier insufficiency. A new route-independent coalitional attainment law proves that a
countable positive carrier exists exactly when finite zero-residual coalitions admit uniform
tightness, equivalently bounded cost in some proper source price; without a carrier every
approximation escapes every proper price. A sharp countermodel shows residual convergence alone
can be a mirage. This is a useful exact Route-P existence interface, but no prime--polar--Gamma
atom family is shown to satisfy it and no local theorem constructs the critical measure.

**Current formal frontier.** The older compact-measure endpoint now has two sharper source-budget
realizations. In the completed-seam lane, curvature normalization removes the quadratic
functional-equation gauge; fourth reciprocal-trace geometry proves the required budget through
`|Im s|<=233/20`, and any finite upper ceiling on the remaining positive tail is equivalent to RH.
In the five-shift lane, critical-feature kernels have exact prime--Gamma decompositions, uniform
nearest-integer and oriented-adjacent transverse quanta, a legal Ward--Green trace, a holomorphic
source majorant, and a removable-extension/critical-line-projector decomposition. Two-prime and
Cayley dephasing then produce a finite-packet exterior carrier with indivisible quantum
`4(96/145)^2`. Coefficient-faithful Lagrange lifting now constructs the literal four-contour prime--
Gamma source for every finite divisor packet and proves its uniform subquantum bound equivalent to
RH. The exterior construction problem is closed, and a generic contour estimate is a receiver.
Pointed four-unit compression is now also closed: radial half is graph gauge and transverse half
contains exact pointed Xi variation. P-G167 also closes finite-sigma complex polarization:
the actual full-Euler columns have exact non-diagonal zeta overlap, the transverse source gives the
pointed prime and continued-Xi reporter at every safe complex node, and the augmented source packet
is an actual positive Hilbert Gram with four-unit boundary energy. P-G168 identifies the
projective two-scale mixed source jet exactly with the cubic Hellinger horizontal separator.
`RoutePFullHardyHalfPlanePolarization.lean` removes the remaining chart artifact: the actual
full-Euler columns exist on the sharp source domain `Re u>-1/2`, all positive projective nodes lie
inside it, and their exact rescaled source overlap tends to `1/(a+b)` at every fixed scale as
`sigma->1+`. The Hardy kernel is therefore a literal Euler-source limit, not a fitted kernel or an
`R->infinity` construction. `RoutePFiniteSigmaCubicEulerTangentGram.lean` constructs the
log-weighted projective Euler columns and genuine half-weighted tangent as actual finite-`sigma`
sources; its exact four-term Gram converges to the cubic Hellinger kernel.
`RoutePCriticalBoundaryOrdinateDiagonalization.lean` then internalizes physical height in the
source and proves equal-ordinate retention and fixed-gap diagonalization.
`RoutePCriticalCollisionScaleKernel.lean` proves the hostile mesoscopic law: gaps
`kappa(sigma-1)` retain a nonzero translation-invariant complex cubic Cauchy kernel. Active-tree
`RoutePCriticalCollisionSchurTrace.lean` proves that this full matrix is the signed first-difference
compression of a doubled Hardy Cauchy Gram's third Schur power. It is positive semidefinite for
arbitrary scaled frequencies, including repetitions, and has exact trace
`sum_i 115/(864*a_i^3)`. Clustering and zero spacing are therefore closed. First open theorems are
a source-derived high-tail ceiling or a completed carrier comparison. Active-tree
`RoutePPrimitiveCollisionTraceBudget.lean` closes the source-side budget: resolved-von-Mangoldt
congruence places the entire arbitrary-frequency kernel on distinct primitive Euler cells, its
diagonal is exactly the existing summable cubic Euler weight, and every finite packet trace is
bounded by one global constant independent of size and frequencies. Remaining carrier comparison
is a coefficient-faithful completed incidence/contraction from primitive Euler cells to Xi-divisor
collision space before divisor expansion. Active-tree P-G175 proves the global source budget is
strictly below `115/108`, the least charge of one strict-right zero. Thus scalar capacity needs no
amplification; only carrier transport is missing. No exact
external theorem located provides either this incidence or the separate high-tail ceiling.

**Current formal state.** P-G92--P-G97 and the full fixed-source Frankenstein seam are
Lean-proved. Lean constructs the local rank-three spectral matrix, normalized trace-class
cofinal sum, shared physical-lattice overlap, globally injective compact operator, coefficient-
faithful actual-Xi Hilbert vectors, and continuous zero-boundary spectrum. Covariant inverse
incidence then builds the summable horizontal carrier. Both fixed theta prime translation and the
actual multiplicity-complete Burnol top conormal produce exactly the same Fisher-weighted reflected
energy imbalance, which vanishes at one retained prime exactly on the critical line. Singular
boundary inversion recovers this carrier but blows up like `1/epsilon`; its cancellation is RH-
equivalent. No external Herglotz, Thorin, co-Poisson, or boundary-triple theorem supplies the
missing source-derived two-sided orientation.

The exterior source layer is now exact through P-G159. `HedenmalmCoefficientFaithfulExteriorSourceContour.lean`
gives the literal four-contour expression, source residues, multiplicity, and fixed-center landing.
`MobiusCriticalComplexPhaseHermitianGram.lean` proves the non-diagonal squarefree source kernel is
Hermitian positive semidefinite and survives the singular Xi graph normalization entry by entry.
P-G160 sharpens the boundary to a saturated four-unit Hardy/Fisher block: adding a
coherent complement mass `d` costs exactly `|d|^2`, so no independent complement budget survives.
P-G161 factors graph-normal tangent through single profile `(1+u)^(-1)r_Xi(1/2)`, whose coefficient
vanishes exactly under RH. Subsequent no-gos show individual first-order isometry is too strong:
scalar completion, differentiable unit-frame overlap, and actual first-jet-flat multiplicative
recovery are all insufficient or RH-equivalent. These results replace old “construct four legs”
task by additive pre-scalar joint conservation and second-order curvature source incidence.

**Formal source advance.** `GammaSewnMobiusApertureStokesInverseTilt.lean` derives an
inverse from the actual pre-Gram Gamma-sewn aperture edge and proves coefficientwise recovery
`log(p)p^(-s(e+1))` and complete Euler landing `-zeta'(s)/zeta(s)` for every `s>1`. The inverse
tends cofinally to zero there but exceeds `log p` and diverges at `s=1`, so ordinary bounded
prime-Hilbert continuation is impossible. `GammaSewnMobiusApertureStokesKotheBoundary.lean`
proves the sharper positive survivor: uniformly on `s>=1` the inverse has at most one prime-log
height step, and its action converges to the critical connection in every Deninger height-Köthe
seminorm. `GammaSewnMobiusApertureStokesMellinResponse.lean` further proves reflection oddness and
path-independent critical crossing of every centered complex Mellin phase. Thus literature can no
longer help by supplying only a bounded-operator limit or phase packaging. The live object is a
Gamma-corrected strong-dual/corona boundary landing that produces the fixed continued finite
response before Gram/KMS completion; then Julia/Cayley saturation and compact measure assembly
remain. All three modules are committed and Lean-proved. Committed P-G63 proves a
fixed nuclear vector cannot be the landing because its Mellin response is entire while the Xi
logarithmic derivative has divisor poles. Its positive survivor is now provisionally constructed
in `GammaSewnMobiusApertureStokesDualCurrent.lean`: the aperture edge is a coefficient functional
continuous against the zeroth Köthe seminorm; transpose Stokes action recovers the safe von
Mangoldt Euler current on every prime-power axis; and its total coefficient mass is the complete
Euler flux for `s>1`. This closes the forced rigged-source direction and uncompleted Euler landing,
not the Gamma/polar completion, Xi divisor singularity, or passive Markov/Thorin response. These
modules are committed and Lean-proved.
`GammaSewnMobiusApertureStokesPositiveChamber.lean` continues the same
source-distribution law to every fixed `s>0`: inverse tilt costs at most one prime factor, full
Kummer action costs two height steps, and transpose action is still exactly the continuous Euler
current even where its scalar total mass is not absolutely convergent. This removes the Euler line
as a source-topology boundary, but supplies neither Gamma/polar completion nor passivity.
`GammaSewnMobiusApertureStokesComplexHalfPlaneCurrent.lean` further adds norm-one Kummer
phase and proves the exact complex Euler transpose law, with the same continuous two-height-step
bound, throughout `Re(s)>0`. This closes vertical Euler continuation; the corona audit below
identifies the remaining completed object more sharply.

`GammaSewnMobiusApertureStokesDeningerTimeCorona.lean` closes compact local
landing. It lifts every compact Deninger-time test to a finite rapid Kummer vector, identifies the
complex dual pairing with exact Euler--Laplace sampling, and proves the physical sewn/phase
transport has the same response. At zero parameter this is the independent Deninger prime
distribution; adding the archimedean local term gives the complete prime--archimedean Lefschetz
trace on every compact support stage. The global unit direction is sharply outside bounded rapid
tests: exact value one on the first `N` prime-two powers forces zeroth Köthe seminorm at least `N`.
Therefore remaining object is singular completed unitization carrying the Gamma/polar corona and
Xi divisor poles, followed by passivity and compact Markov/Thorin assembly. Compact local trace and
vertical Euler continuation are no longer open.

`GammaSewnMobiusApertureStokesCoronaOrderNoGo.lean` further rules out the
obvious positive completion. Finite prime-two indicators lie in the coefficientwise real unit
interval, but their zero-parameter current is exactly `N log 2`; hence that current is unbounded on
the order interval and has no finite positive order-unit extension. A viable completion must use
parameter-dependent resolvent/reflection order or a renormalized signed channel. Route P's open
core is therefore renormalized/reflection-relative singular completion, not generic passivity.

The subsequent committed P-G73--P-G78 chain makes the remaining source geometry sharper. It
proves first-information depletion quantitatively insufficient, separates reciprocal-prime Hardy
and Deninger topologies, falsifies orientation-symmetrized Pluecker complement averaging, places
completed valuation states in the Koethe dual rather than Hilbert source, and constructs a
continuous positive weak kernel through which non-Cauchy prime prefixes converge to exact
Gamma-valuation Szego covariance. P-G79--P-G91 closes the finite/cofinal physical
geometry. Chirality is positive for every ordered prime pair and depth; finite packets assemble;
complete-graph incidence is nonsummable; the adjacent-prime path is summable with Schur fraction
in `(1/2,1)`; its local edge Gram is not global covariance; and cumulative inverse incidence
recovers the all-prime weak kernel. Applying inverse incidence before Gram/scalar depletion gives
a positive continuous global reduced kernel. Applying one uniform scalar through the compact
reciprocal-gap pushforward fails: its prime-two depth-zero first moment alone exceeds the complete
target moment. P-G90 closes the apparent node-dependent repair as well: every physical local
residual is at least `1/5`, so any nonnegative coefficientwise diagonal transfer retaining that
floor already overshoots at prime two, depth zero. Formal P-G91 retains the full covariance of the four-
label selected-pair incidence vector in each physical six-minor packet instead of collapsing it to
one Fisher scalar. Prime-power uniqueness makes the four rates distinct and all six probabilities
positive; the covariance is positive semidefinite, has kernel exactly the constant direction, and
has a nonzero off-diagonal entry in every physical packet. This is the first source-forced local
matrix with negative cross entries available for cancellation and pole movement.

P-G92 performs the first literal spectralization. P-G93 fixes the source-normalized block
coefficient and constructs the positive Schatten-one direct sum. P-G94 descends packet copies to
the shared prime-depth lattice, and P-G95 proves global injectivity by classifying the only formal
kernel as a non-square-summable Stokes-gap section. P-G96 admits both actual-Xi reporter
orientations; P-G97 proves zero is continuous spectrum rather than an eigenvalue. The later
Frankenstein modules identify every reporter coordinate with the fixed-prime translation/Burnol
conormal imbalance and prove post hoc anomaly cancellation equivalent to RH.

First missing theorem is therefore not global assembly or another moment identity. For one fixed
ordinary prime, derive before P-G95 from completed Euler--Gamma source geometry the equality
`p^(-(2 Re(q)-1))=p^(2 Re(q)-1)` on every actual divisor mode, or an equivalent two-sided
contractivity law in a singular moving pre-Hilbert fibre. Extra all-prime summability is not
needed. Bounded target inversion, abstract intertwining, reflection symmetry, or declaring the
anomaly a boundary is ruled out by the formal spectrum and equivalence theorems. This seam is a
Route-P/R/L bridge, not a separate route.

`HedenmalmThetaGreenSquareBoundaryLaw.lean` supplies a concrete C1/P opening.
For the source-built theta inverse `B`, singular positive metric `G`, and universal pinned
rank-two current `R_2`, it gives

```text
G B^2 = B^* G B + i R_2 B.
```

It also proves that strictly positive real nonzero spectrum of `B^2` is equivalent to real
nonzero spectrum of `B`, RH, and—through existing capstones—the compact Markov and Thorin
properties. It now also proves pointwise neutrality of the source-built Green boundary current on
every multiplicity-exact divisor mode is equivalent to each of those endpoints. Candidate local
contribution is the source-specific identification of the full Green-square obstruction with
`iR_2B`; elementary square-realness, generic positive bulk, and endpoint equivalence alone are not
novelty claims. This remains a receiver: it does not construct the P measure or prove direct
factorization of every P producer through C1, because its Markov/Thorin equivalences pass through
RH.

`HedenmalmThetaGreenReflectionPairNoGo.lean`
proves the reflected-pair shortcut is vacuous: functional-equation reflection preserves theta root
energy and negates the signed boundary flux, so pair neutrality holds unconditionally. Same-fibre
neutrality conjoined with that automatic law remains exactly RH. External reflection symmetry
therefore cannot replace a source theorem neutralizing each divisor fibre before aggregation.

The completed Burnol/Hedenmalm chain identifies fixed-prime translation excess, Ward/Krein energy,
Dirac/radial work, cofinal Sonin escape, and singular Green/Weyl sewing as scalarizations of one
horizontal displacement. Entire finite-packet gauges have no rigidity; a cofinal reciprocal gauge
or same-fibre neutrality is already RH-equivalent. The sharper first-trace chain now retains the
unsquared divisor current before that scalarization. The squarefree first-trace anomaly continues
to the valuation-one von Mangoldt current plus analytic higher valuations. Its covariant moving-
horizon finite part is positive and summable but reflection-even; its sharp polar finite part is
faithful but sign-indefinite. The oriented current has a quantized neutral jump `+/-4`, and the
canonical completed polar countercurrent cancels that jump, leaving a continuous finite remainder.
Hedenmalm root flux factors as `-1/2` times reflected source entropy times a strictly positive root
energy, so flux neutrality is exactly critical-line neutrality. The actual theta source has an
infinite coordinate orbit, excluding every fixed finite-dimensional source-port repair.

The exterior lane now avoids post hoc fibre orientation. A three-clock source projector selects
only line-reflected partners and collapses the surviving minors to a Pluecker square. The inverse
transverse Green factor is the boundary of a source-Cayley Abel orbit and is removable on the
critical line after exteriorization. One fixed prime translation of the Hedenmalm source supplies
the horizontal Ward clock. Fixed features are declared before divisor selection, and fixed-center
exterior energy equals transverse energy. The affine companion dephases unequal ordinates and
records a multiplicity-`M` fibre by the exact determinant `-4M^2`, so phase and multiplicity cannot
cancel the off-line quantum.

The exterior tangent is now completely classified through the scalarization wall. Complex
non-diagonal source Gram survives graph normalization. Four units are sharp, and a coherent escaped
mass `d/(1+u)` costs exactly `|d|^2`. The division-free graph-normal tangent is the rank-one profile
`(1+u)^(-1)r_Xi(1/2)`, whose coefficient vanishes exactly under RH. Under false RH ordinary Schur

A further paper theorem awaiting specialist audit resolves what root division can and cannot add.

has an independent odd zero-energy twin. Under full-ground nonnegativity this gives
`dim ker>=1+2E`; negative roots with positive terminal mass are impossible, a zero root creates
odd degeneracy, and simple full ground forces every squared root strictly positive. Thus the extra
directions are explicit parity lifts, not unclassified source data. Distinct-root descendants
remain ordinary Lagrange orthogonality, and the explicit radical pencil falsifies polar cyclicity.
This parity companion is dependency-closed on paper and novelty-unverified; literal hard-edge

### Cross-route receiver — reflection self-intersection charge [NOT A NEW ROUTE]

Lean splits horizontal collision charge into canonical reflection self-intersection and
accidental same-ordinate collisions. The canonical term is exactly twice the sum of squared
right-half-plane multiplicities, equals the squared off-line order, and is bounded by the full
collision charge. Therefore RH is equivalent to this canonical charge being `<2` for all, one
cofinal, or every eventual cutoff. Multiplicity-copy incidence is exact. This sharpens the
receiver by removing accidental collisions, but it is computed after divisor formation and has no
pre-zero source producer. External zero-density, pair-correlation, or universality results do not
supply the required unconditional two-unit bound, so the construction does not instantiate a
twelfth route or select a literature-to-Lean theorem.
