p-adic/Eisenstein branch now has an integral specialization. The finite-unitary
adjoint-autocorrelation law and its energy-matched specialization are now Lean-proved:
every nonzero traceless autonomous Hamiltonian retains full generator autocorrelation at every
time, while the Haar half-energy tail is at most `4/(D^2-1)`. None has a typed Xi source handoff.

The Lean-proved longitudinal--transverse formation law supplies a sharper shared no-go.
The complete source splits into its scalar pole-unit response and zero-codiagonal radical; every
intervention output is exactly marked-section action plus a mixing defect. Radical preservation
is equivalent to zero mixing and can only rescale the pre-existing longitudinal response, while
arbitrary endpoint-dependent transverse perturbations are invisible to the complete-shell scale
ratio. This identifies a necessary mechanism for any new route—a source-generated nonzero mixing
gate before divisor specialization—but does not instantiate one.

Two generic modules are now Lean-proved. Cofinal inverse capacity identifies
the null space of a limiting infimal-lift seminorm with the vertical fibre of a closed graph and
characterizes closability by nondegeneracy. Torus-invariant-field rigidity identifies the
invariants of a character-separating scaling group with the velocity field and its intersection
with the palette rational-function field with the constants. These sharpen completion and
scale-symmetry no-gos; neither has an arithmetic Xi handoff.

Critical-cover algebra now proves three sharper receivers: full actual geometric monodromy makes
the source-field stabilizer maximal and forbids proper intermediate fields below it; one shared
nonzero coprime tangent identifies two critical fibre states; and a Schur reduction with at most
two colliding sheets forces inertia to be identity or one swap and yields a transposition when
inertia is nontrivial. The formalized Schur-place integral-closure model constructs the contracted
maximal prime, localization/valuation-ring equivalence, ideal inertia, residue characteristic
zero, and inertia-cardinality/ramification-index identity. These
results have no located exact external collision as one source-specific package, but their full
monodromy/collision premises do not orient Xi zeros and create no route-level literature import.

The fixed sinc-reporter theory now realizes every positive horizon exponent `2r` and inverse
exponent `1/(2r)` by `exp(-|t|^(2r))`. A distinguished or rational exponent therefore cannot by
itself classify a source law or select an RH architecture.

The cofinal viability layer proves that compact observer fibres and closed decreasing horizons
promote all finite lifts to one global source lift; false finite survival forces escape from every
compact set and divergence of every proper source cost. Compact legal-control fibres also yield
the greatest endogenous viable fixed law. No arithmetic observer/horizon or Xi handoff is
instantiated.

The symbolic-execution mechanism theorem adds a decisive promotion no-go. Executable repair
implies local symbolic admission, and the converse holds for one failure kernel. On the triangle,
uniform weights are locally admitted at unbounded grades but admit no decomposition into legal
grade-one repairs, while their normalized profile and parity observer remain constant. Local
Hall/failure inequalities plus bounded observer data therefore cannot replace an actual compatible
source history. No arithmetic repair family or Xi handoff is instantiated.

The primitive-execution refinement makes the required history sharper. Every positive locally
admitted repair decomposes canonically into primitive repairs, and bounded grammar is equivalent
to bounded primitive degree. Complete-complement laws have primitive degree `k-1`, so every
bounded grammar misses an explicit cofinal law. Primitive degree spectrum survives relabeling and
failure-antichain quotient, while an exact primitive ray has periodic projective/cyclic observers
but unbounded exact grade. A viable arithmetic instance therefore needs cofinal primitive spectrum
or unbounded grammar. No prime--Gamma repair family or Xi handoff is instantiated.

Positive observer transport preserves both admission and execution and cannot create an execution
gap, but a noninjective triangle observer can erase a real gap while manufacturing false positive
overlap. Observer collapse is information loss, not a source repair.

The current constraint no-gos are equally source-diagnostic. The Riemann radial slope profile is
cross-prime flat for every spectral parameter, and pure two-factor data have a scaling torsor with
no natural transverse line; neither flatness nor unframed factorization detects the critical line.
Completion can also forget raw flatness: scalar chain-additive divergences reduce to potential
differences and fail naturality across the basic completion diagram, while an oriented universal
provenance class survives. This validates label-retaining/framed targets such as Route R's but
does not supply their arithmetic annihilation. The quadratic-collision model also gives the sharp
decoder norm `sqrt(2)/|c|` and constant discriminant--conditioning product `sqrt(2)`, proving that
the singular recovery blow-up is structural rather than an RH orientation law.

Further formal capstones show that static conservativity does not determine deformation law,
neutral differential fusion can create full transcendence with vanishing positive cotangent
obstruction, two distinct positive absolute grades force zero radial drift, separating valuation
coordinates preserve every off-critical diagonal defect under completion, and transport
amplification diverges as triangle edge accuracy tends to zero. The quadratic mate law now has
exact different `delta-1` and discriminant `(delta-1)^2`, both vanishing precisely at `delta=1`;
this locates but does not force the collision wall. These exact laws rule out several generic
promotions but supply no arithmetic producer.

Additional Lean capstones identify scalar, magnitude, and support as dependent shadows whose
product cannot select; exclude a bounded fixed-code observer from reproducing unbounded wormhole
length; and identify difference, discriminant/Fitting energy, support kernel, and conditioning as
four shadows of one quadratic law. Question/relation balance and the
rank/depth/orientation/discriminant transmutation dictionary are exact. Longer words for one fixed
action never recreate freedom already killed by that action; new selection requires a changed
action, module, quotient, admission law, or source. Full/projection clone and top/bottom
congruence extremes are now nontrivial and transport-stable, confirming that faithful re-encoding
preserves rather than creates the law lattice. These are no-go classifications, not routes.

New boundary and first-variation laws sharpen this classification. The commutative Shilov module
identifies metric boundary ideals exactly with closed norming sets. For function systems satisfying
unit, star-closure, and point separation, Choquet boundary is norming and contained in every closed
boundary, and Shilov boundary is the least closed boundary and equals its closure. New modules now
identify the Shilov ideal as greatest boundary ideal, construct the surjective C*-envelope
restriction with exactly that kernel, prove Stone--Weierstrass generation, characterize boundary
representations by unique extension, and identify the affine-function-system envelope with the
extreme boundary. The full commutative model is therefore closed. No arithmetic Hedenmalm/Weil
source system, coefficient-faithful reader, or genuinely noncommutative envelope is instantiated.
Independently,
every functional of a complete off-line splitting quadruple is even in horizontal displacement;
its first derivative at the critical locus vanishes identically, so a first-order symmetric
detector cannot orient RH and the second central jet is the first possible signal.

The new Shilov tower modules prove the upstairs deficit formula and the exact
Mittag--Leffler-to-`lim^1` implication. They also show both sides of the independence boundary:
arbitrary prescribed link cokernels can coexist with vanishing `lim^1`, while a non-surjective
polynomial tower has nonvanishing `lim^1`. Coherent-lift criteria and stagewise-density recovery of
all coherent families are formal as well. The associated operator-system layer now contains
matrix orderings, completely positive maps, scalar-to-matrix order-unit promotion, matrix level
norms, the Ruan direct-sum law, and isometric compression contractivity. Its locally C* companion
constructs complete closed projective limits with coordinate C*-seminorm identities and a stable
bounded part. Local deficits therefore
do not determine global derived obstruction, and generic tower hypotheses do not give universal
acyclicity. This is a real receiver advance, but it supplies no arithmetic Xi operator system,
arithmetic/noncommutative C*-envelope instance, trace-filtered fracture, or annihilator.

Constitutive additions prove exact future-law interiors, conservative carrier base change,
descent-breaking grammar extension, diagonal reporter genesis, relational-composition
classification, and the dimension/positivity limits of incomplete process tomography. They say
where a new observer law must enter. New mechanism theorems add an exact attainment/escape law for
countable positive carriers and two repair laws: one dependent repair can leave semantic range
fixed while birthing a positive minimal circulation and destroying scalar coercivity, while two
individually nonviable repairs can cross an exact collective spectral threshold. These suggest
tight source pricing and irreducibly collective repair as possible producer primitives, but no
arithmetic source or Xi handoff is instantiated. A formal Arens-space example now proves two strict hereditary
native sequential-repair layers can be necessary even while ordinary closure is already
stationary. It is a genuine endogenous-completion mechanism, but instantiates no arithmetic source
or Xi handoff.

**Route classification.** These theorems clarify admissible producer categories but have no typed
handoff to a live route's first unresolved arithmetic premise. Compact-carrier representation does
not build P's positive source measure, and coalitional attainment supplies only an exact
tightness/pricing criterion; dense prime tangents do not provide R/L singular
annihilation or U adaptive sign; Bures minimax and the formal zeta-gas laws do not provide E/T
all-prime cancellation. Riemann pullback is an RH-equivalent selector, not an unconditional
producer. Strongest positive design survivor is an irreducible unbounded-arity interaction or
cofinal future-sensitive reporter invisible to every fixed proper face. No arithmetic source
instance or unconditional Xi handoff is present, so no independent route or literature-to-Lean
action follows from this batch.

## 3. Secondary criteria

Li–Keiper, Nyman–Beurling/Báez–Duarte, Salem, Hardy, Colombeau, theta-infinity, mollifier,
Burnol co-Poisson, OPUC, determinant-line, spectral-flow, de Bruijn–Newman, Jensen-polynomial,
Lagarias, and related criteria add coordinates rather than source producers.

Yang's August 2026
[Friedrichs-angle theorem](https://doi.org/10.1016/j.jmaa.2026.130494) proves that RH implies zero
Friedrichs angle for two localized Nyman–Beurling pairs, while also proving unconditional strict
inclusions among the corresponding subspace sums; its completeness proposition gives another RH
equivalence. The located result supplies neither a converse angle criterion nor the missing source
estimate of a live route. It is current Hilbert-space SOTA, but gives no frontier-leverage Lean
import.

The local `QuantitativeNymanBeurling` module contributes only the exact scalar Hardy-kernel cost
model: normalized kernel and floor identities, positivity, inverse-height upper degradation, and a
reciprocal complementary-length inequality. Its theorem signatures do not contain concrete
fractional-part generators, Mellin transforms, the global closure equivalence, or RH. Thus neither
the local module nor Yang's theorem supplies the decisive approximation estimate; no beyond-SOTA
source theorem is claimed here.

Michalowski's July 2026
[uniform cubic wedge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16795) proves consecutive Xi-coefficient Toeplitz
minor positivity `D_{r,k}>0` for every `r≥2` and `k≥10^18 r^3`. It explicitly leaves
`k<10^18 r^3`, especially the RH-critical regime `k=O(r)`, open. Local Lean does not contain the
literal Xi-minor statement: `PolyaFrequencyIsTheHypothesis` transports only an assumed generic
`PF ↔ Reality`, while `HankelToeplitzWindow` proves a factorial window and generic order-two
log-convexity. The external theorem therefore supplies a precise secondary frontier boundary, not
a live-route source estimate. Its proof depends on directed-rounding Arb certificates; no import is
permitted under the no-computation rule. Formalizing only its exact q-Pascal identities would not
narrow the complementary critical cone.

The 2026 Salem improper-integral criterion and Liflandsky one-way Möbius implication are already
formalized. Hedenmalm's normalized boundary problem and generic Hardy evaluation obstruction are
formalized. Exact absent literature packages are:

- Li's full analytic criterion, not merely a finite or sequence-level abstraction;
- the concrete Nyman–Beurling/Báez–Duarte generators, Mellin theorem, and global closure criterion;
- Hardy's 2026 concrete evaluation functions and their density/obstruction theorems;
- the 2026 automorphic theta-at-infinity carrier and its density consequences;
- Burnol's evaluator/co-Poisson completeness and exact dual systems;
- the concrete Suzuki, CCM, Inoue, Deninger, Groskin, and Burnol/semilocal theorems named above.

Full Li, Nyman–Beurling, Hardy, and theta-at-infinity formalizations improve corpus completeness but
do not narrow a live source premise beyond an equivalent positivity, density, or closure wall. They
are therefore secondary to the concrete operator/carrier imports. Yang's unconditional strict
subspace inclusions sharpen Nyman–Beurling geometry but do not change this ordering. Colombeau
remains low priority: no located unconditional theorem improves a route's first open core.

## 4. Retired routes and external SOTA

No primary source found by the cutoff revives a retired mechanism at the quantifier level killed in
the corpus. External work supports several of the same boundaries:

- finite/local positivity does not extrapolate automatically to complete Weil positivity;
- scalar, finite-moment, fixed-degree, and finite-rank data cannot control an unrestricted source;
- Suzuki's conjectural entire limit is not established, and the literal `z²ξ/ξ′` target has real
  poles independently of RH;
- CCM and Suzuki numerical convergence is not an analytic finite-to-infinite theorem;
- generic Julia/Gram capacity cannot overcome the literal first-clock bad diagonal, whose coherent
  energy is locally proved unbounded uniformly in scale;
- Burnol's qualitative completeness cannot change the full finite-height Sonin conductor's proved
  affine-logarithmic order against a positive-linear source schedule;
- zero-density, critical-line proportions, gap statistics, and large-value estimates do not
  eliminate every off-line zero;
- criteria assuming RH, simple zeros, localization, independence, or arbitrary-length moment
  bounds cannot supply those hypotheses.

Latest Lean guardrails strengthen this verdict. `SymmetryCagedTranslationFreeness.lean` preserves
functional equation, Schwarz reality, and arbitrary finite jet passport while zero-free gauges
make translation differential algebra free; critical-line and off-line divisors remain
indistinguishable. `PositiveSourceLoewnerBudget.lean` proves unrestricted nonnegative source
weights force any fixed uniform kernel budget to vanish pointwise, while a mass cap reduces budget
to pointwise domination. Phase-seed collision modules give exact concentration, but
  `SpectralCollisionFunctional.lean` now also constructs matrix-continuous capped moment
regularization bounding and converging to collision at simple spectrum; nevertheless
`PhaseGraphSobolevBlindness.lean` proves singular return graph cannot be recovered by subcritical
Sobolev control. `PositiveStieltjesPoleFirstAtomCounterexample.lean`,
`N1SignedBulkExteriorMonotonicity.lean`, and `OutlierQuadratureCountermodel.lean` rule out low-order
Stieltjes/Christoffel/fixed-moment propagation through signed boundary atoms. These are structural
no-gos with positive survivors—symmetry-sensitive source coupling, magnitude-aware Loewner
budgets, singular incidence control, and moving endpoint flux—not new routes.
`SourceNormalizationBudgetGates.lean` further proves finite exact normalizations leave recession:
one-sided fixed budget forces direction PSD and two-sided budget forces direction zero.
`HTGeometry/RealAxisKernelPositivityNonselector.lean` gives exact `3x3` counterexample whose scalar
real-axis kernel is `1+t^2+t^4>0` while form has two negative squares.
`FiniteSquarefreeEulerFunctionalEquation.lean` strengthens finite complement result: under exact
finite functional equation local squares `a_p^2=p` are forced and sufficient, epsilon is explicit,
and every finite-product zero lies on `Re(s)=1/2`. This positive rigidity still exposes terminal
boundary: infinite Euler source has no top/cofinite Dirichlet coefficient, so finite functional-
equation complements cannot converge into missing all-prime selector.

Prescribed-gap synthesis supplies a stronger nonselection theorem at paper level. Any stationary
ergodic positive mean-one gap law is realized by real zeros of even exact-order-one
Laguerre--Pólya function with maximal translation differential-algebraic independence; multiplying
by positive-on-real even polynomial adds off-real quartet without changing real gaps or profield.
Lean proves ergodic/generic gap-sequence and block-law-stability components. Thus exact order,
functional symmetry, local GUE-type gaps, and maximal HT cannot be RH producer without pointed
source/operator admission. No external gap-statistics theorem repairs this logical blindness.

Gaber's July preprint
[Euler's ℓ-totients and Riemann hypothesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26114) is rejected as a
source. Its Theorem 4.3 claims under RH an `O(x)` error for every fixed `ℓ`; at `ℓ=1` this is the
ordinary summatory Euler totient error and contradicts Montgomery's unconditional
`Ω±(x sqrt(log log x))` theorem
([Fluctuations in the mean of Euler's phi function](https://doi.org/10.1007/BF02837826)). The
preprint's stronger converse hypothesis is not a matching equivalence and cannot repair the false
forward implication. No theorem from it is a formalization candidate.

The growing-order reverse-Bessel finite-prime mechanism is also retired. Classical Bessel
polynomial theory begins with Krall and Frink's
[1949 construction](https://doi.org/10.1090/S0002-9947-1949-0028473-1), and later work gives broad
large-order asymptotics, including Wong and Zhang's
[generalized Bessel expansions](<https://doi.org/10.1016/S0377-0427(97)00131-3>). The special-function
ancestry is therefore not a local novelty claim. The corpus-specific result is the exact
squared-Xi consequence: normalized reverse-Bessel weights cancel Euler damping at every fixed
prime, forcing any surviving argument into a moving nonsummable prime window. That theorem and its
positive integral carrier are already formalized; the located literature supplies no completed
prime--Gamma moving-window comparison to import.

The corpus nevertheless contributes sharper positive survivors: exact fixed-width tomography,
refinement conservation, source boundary charges, light-cone Gram decompositions, quantitative
leakage thresholds, compactification residual classifications, normal-cone diagonals, and explicit
rate deficits. No exact external collision was located for most of these survivors.

## 5. Disproof frontier

The local corpus has unusually explicit typed disproof certificates: negative finite Weil/Hankel
forms, first-clock or rectangular divergence, positive Ward index, nonzero SEM charge, source-sector
failures, and right-half divisor/ladder witnesses. Their logical sufficiency is formalized; none is
produced unconditionally.

External literature supplies no rigorously certified off-critical zeta zero and no unconditional
negative certificate. Numerical verification of many critical-line zeros is evidence against, not a
disproof route. Claimed proof/disproof manuscripts are not imported without independent complete
proof audit.

The most source-explicit local negative route is the growing-order squared-Xi radial jet: one
negative completed reverse-Bessel source jet disproves RH, orders through eighteen are positive,
and fixed-prime localization is formally impossible. Classical Bessel-polynomial literature
supplies special-function ancestry but no cancellation-preserving moving-prime-window comparison
with the completed Gamma/polar source. No external import advances that open core.
## 6. Formalization and import priorities

External material should be imported only where it shortens a current first open theorem.

1. **Route T — highest-confidence import.** Formalize one explicit Ingham/Hoheisel
   density-to-prime theorem using already-proved zero-density exponent
   `82571/118590` with squared-log loss and explicit `1/80` threshold room. Required statement is eventual existence of a prime in
   (x,x+x^(7/10)], with constants and smoothing compatible with physical current. Detector
   endpoint, contour, horizontal, and zero-count work must not be repeated.
2. **Route U — odd-boundary admission/Julia charge.** Exact finite ancestry, radial flux, and
   parity-doubled root-jet rigidity, normal-family extraction, real-rooted limit, and root division
   are formal; dominated null/positivity passage is also formal. Exclude or orient admitted boundary
   state by odd-sector/prime--Gamma sign or degenerate-birth contradiction; equivalent targets are no bounded super-apex, positive first Hausdorff
   flux, or no cofinal Julia-normalized atom. Ordinary Padé capacity and unweighted jets are dead.
3. **Route G — Morse exhaustion and residual injectivity.** Wall generator and residual Evans
   reduction are closed. Prove literal screw-source/Krein--Langer aperture-index equality and exact
   infinite-boundary reconstruction; then prove source-defined `2L` residual map injective and
   orient return.
4. **Route D2 — no known bridge to import.** Polynomial-horizon Littlewood deficit is already
   formal. Needed continuation through Euler wall is not supplied by located zero-free-region or
   pretentious literature.
5. **Routes L and P — avoid generic categorical/operator imports.** Big-Witt algebra, singular
   graph carrier, and two-sheet mass/orientation handoff to CCM/Sonin readouts are closed. P raw
   finite sign is fully classified and RH-equivalent. Needed result is cofinal source-faithful
   completed adaptive correction plus selected Stokes annihilation with primitive-budget control.
   Generic cyclic homology, Douglas factorization, or operator shorting would only restate receiver.
6. **Route O — identify signed ground mode, then higher ladder.** Ground separation `mu_c->1`,
   fixed-window Gaussian convergence, uniform total mass, and centre-mass asymptotic are formal for
   selected band family. Prove cross-spectral ordering to identify signed Fourier mode zero; then
   higher-mode min--max/rung-index identification and whole-ground exhaustion.
7. **CCM-FU and C1 — no more finite jets/determinants.** Whole CCM source matrix, cubic response,
   and second-Abel/Cesàro completeness are closed; theta determinant/multiplicity and Gauss cubic
   convergence are closed. Work must target the Cesàro-linear literal prime-minus-polar source law
   or source-derived complete spectral sign.
8. **CCM-UR — common completed-current lower law.** Lean already supplies transform factorization,
   signed correction, and literal completed-source response. Formalize only remaining no-loss
   lower-bound/Landau handoff, then attack signed lower control of positive two-exponential
   smoothing. Generic coisometry, scalar Pick, or Fock reformulation is receiver work.
9. **CCM-RF — adjacent-block lower floor.** Prove one source-first positive cross-scale synthesis
   giving an eventual lower bound for the fixed-width adjacent critical-Mellin block imbalance.
   Tent/Haar geometry and the RH endpoint are already formal; absolute prime/polar estimates cannot
   replace joint cancellation.
10. **CCM-EP — boundary transfer, not more finite spectra.** Paired-zero Pick kernel, PSD-limit
   closure, aperture defect, periodic-background decay/indefiniteness, and relative Herglotz gate
   are formal. Work must construct dephased actual-CCM logarithmic-derivative convergence across
   `Re(w)=1/2` or signed/operator-valued background covariance; more real finite spectra do not
   cross boundary.
11. **SP-E — identify already-stable physical determinant with Xi.** Literal physical-overlap
    Pluecker operator, source normalization, positivity, faithfulness, Schatten-one class, finite
    negative-real-rooted determinants, locally uniform entire limit, and canonical ordinary
    determinant trace restoration are formal. Direct `det2` identification is first-jet impossible.
    Prove source trace equals half first theta moment and compare all higher ordinary Fredholm
    coefficients with Xi; if higher identity fails, construct
    continuous-zero-compatible renormalized boundary transfer. Do not revisit amplitude-eight or
    local metric repair.
12. **TC-HS — half-step transport.** Formalize paper defect/reflection theorem, then construct
    source-native half-step or density neutralizing reflection coefficient before divisor reading.

Computational zero certificates, numerical CCM/Suzuki convergence, and conditional critical
statements remain excluded by corpus no-computation and unconditional-proof rules.

## 7. Present conclusion

Current Lean corpus changes route frontiers, but not route count or RH status.

- CCM-FU now has a second-Abel/Cesàro RH endpoint strictly broader than every fixed pointwise
  subcubic envelope. Its first open theorem is linear growth of one Cesàro primitive of the literal
  third-grade prime-minus-polar cumulative source.
- G now has positive simple even chamber, finite-packet Morse charge, literal wall-generator and
  boundary-Laplace control, and a square residual Evans determinant after infinite-boundary
  warning. Open problems are literal aperture-Morse exhaustion and completed reconstruction/
  determinant nonvanishing/orientation.
- O seed science is paper-closed. Lean closes construction, target normalization, primitives, polar
  charge, physical diagonal spectrum, exact-solution off-diagonal/high-row summation, defect mass,
  two-scale carrier, and abstract assembly. Literal normalized-source dictionary is certification
  debt. Actual selected ground separation tends to one; centre-normalized profile converges on
  fixed windows and normalized total mass/centre-mass asymptotic are formal. Cross-spectral ordering
  identifying signed mode zero now precedes higher constrained-prolate ladder/mode-index theorem
  and RH-bearing whole-ground exhaustion.
- P now has an explicit finite mixed prime--Gamma source sequence whose negative energy is
  equivalent to `not RH`; every finite raw energy is nonpositive and exact positive correction is
  twice fundamental-symmetry gap. Raw nonnegativity is RH-equivalent, not producer. First open
  source theorem is cofinal completed adaptive correction/selected annihilation with
  primitive-budget control. Two-sheet Gamma-six carrier already preserves centered orientation and
  gives exact CCM/Sonin readouts from literal ordinary-prime Meyer--Tate source cells; it does not
  prove cofinal budget/annihilation theorem.
- T analytic detector chain ends in fixed-gap zero-density below 7/10, and moving Gabriel geometry
  has exact 31/10 coefficient with 7/100 room. Actual critical fourth, mollifier, detector-error,
  and translated Gaussian budgets now have polylogarithmic cost. Density-to-prime supply and
  current composition remain.
- U degree escape is strongly compactified in Lean: normalized nonpositive bounded-scaled-degree
  hard-edge family is totally bounded in `L2`, with nonzero norm-one subsequential limit; exact
  Cayley angular momentum, all-order descendant bounds, and finite-exponential-type identification
  are formal, as is conditional full parity-jet inertia: repeated roots force parity-doubled ground
  directions and simple full ground forces simple positive roots. Cayley normality, real-rooted
  nonzero limit, dominated null/positivity passage, and root division are formal under exact
  hypotheses. Exact Hausdorff inertia traces negative terminal root to finite super-apex ancestry.
  The full moving-centre Padé resolvent is one-monomial and its poles move monotonically, but this
  generic positive-chamber flow cannot anchor the absolute boundary. Lean also proves the
  highest-moment absolute-divisor majorant diverges at critical mode/height scale, so phase or
  common-operator correlation is mandatory.
  First target is exclusion/orientation of admitted boundary state through odd-sector/prime--Gamma
  sign or degenerate-birth contradiction; equivalent super-apex/flux/Julia-residue exclusion and
  cross Gamma balance remain.
- D2 gains divergent safe-line deficit on every polynomial frequency horizon but still cannot cross
  Euler wall.
- L gains exact Euler-factor/Witt algebra and singular one-leg graph completion. Canonical
  prime-harmonic labelled source packets now generate vertical trace with exact Xi Green weighting;
  actual Gamma readout and two-sheet orientation handoff are exact; canonical selected
  non-universal Stokes annihilation remains open.
- C1 gains cubic propagated-source convergence without sign on full deviation sector.
- Q now has canonical measurable plateau quantizer from literal theta law, positive fibres, and all
  moments, and it can be chosen nondecreasing; first-moment excess still vanishes exactly under RH.
  A separated finite continued-Cauchy prime–Gamma packet sequence now reconstructs orientation
  energy unconditionally. Nonnegative source recurrence at arbitrarily late indices, equivalently
  reverse Cassini boundary sign, remains; this weaker cofinal gate is already RH-equivalent.

Generic moving-divisor additions close a separate false lead: every finite actual jet probe is a
submersion, dense residual even carriers have full translation differential-algebraic freeness,
and this remains compatible with every prescribed stationary ergodic positive mean-one gap law.
Maximal jet/translation genericity therefore cannot distinguish the Riemann divisor or supply RH.
Likewise every finite nonzero solution-jet prescription is locally realizable by some
logarithmic-derivative pair `E'=H E`, and induced full jet substitution is injective. Generic
logarithmic-derivative differential algebra therefore supplies no hidden divisor selector; full
jet independence of base family transfers to logarithmic-derivative solutions.
`HTGeometry/RationalJetShift.lean` closes all-order denominator recursion for rational gauge shifts;
this is bookkeeping, not arithmetic selection.

These shifts strengthen central corpus diagnosis: missing mathematics is constitutive, not
representational. Source already carries labels, depth, reflection, multiplicity, and exact
arithmetic coefficients. Routes stop when one must control complete ground/spectrum, cross a
singular boundary without scalarizing orientation, continue positive Euler data through its wall,
or convert zero density into physical prime supply.

No newly audited Lean module supplies a new independent route. CCM-HG's boundary relative trace,
CCM-GS's ground-separating incidence, CCM-UR's completed-current smoothing, CCM-RF's rectangular
adjacent-block floor, and CCM-EP's dephased Euler--Pick boundary transfer are candidate openings.
CCM-RF has an exact Lean RH endpoint and CCM-UR an exact Lean source weld, but neither has its
signed source producer; both share CCM-FU's completed current and therefore do not increase
independent route count. Causal-Pick quotient and diagonal-Fock constructions are exact receivers,
not routes.
Sonin three-grade algebra now strengthens CCM-HG without changing that classification: compressed
commutator is internal defect plus polarized leakage, exact resolvent correction factors through
one edge commutator, and scalar observability deficit is paid by relative negative index. Closed
principal--relative carrier, rigged edge trace, finite-part descent, and cofinal index-preserving
transport remain absent.
SP-E and TC-HS are additional non-CCM candidates. SP-E has an unconditional source-native stable
entire determinant; missing gate is completed-Xi arithmetic identification or renormalized boundary
transfer, not metric or cofinal assembly. TC-HS has paper-level weighted reflection defect but lacks
source-native half-step neutralization.
Periodic-pencil intrinsic dimension two, Cubic Ward/Hellinger, CCM–E, CCM–P–L two-sheet, and
formation-completion seams remain route-design information rather than additional live routes.
External SOTA
provides useful bridge technology, especially Route T density-to-prime transfer and Route U
boundary-state classification, but no located unconditional theorem closes any source-level RH
premise. RH and its negation remain unproved.
